# Rights warnings report

Generated 2026-11-21 05:51 by `scripts/rights_report.py` from `data/media.json`. Do not edit by hand. Read-only: no file or record was changed and nothing was fetched from the web.

Every row is a media file that `gta6-hq --validate` flags as `rights-unconfirmed`: it is shown in the app (`approval_status: approved`) but its `rights_status` is not `approved`. Recommended actions come only from what each record states. **No file here has a recorded licence**, and nothing here is legal advice.

## Check against the validator

`gta6-hq --validate` reports 983 warnings in all, 982 of them `rights-unconfirmed`; this report has 982 rows. The two lists match file for file. The other 1 warning(s) are other checks and are not part of this report.

### Records that contradict themselves

These name an outside source URL but also a `derived_from` parent file. They are treated as derivatives (the more cautious reading) until the record is corrected.

- `images/vehicles/speedboat.jpg`: `derived_from` is `images/pier-showdown.jpg`, but `source` gives https://www.rockstargames.com/VI/media/screenshots
